SR² Hand documentation · updated 2026-10-30

Result pages of the SR² Hand documentation by topic and date

The 107 dated result pages (268 MB) under docs/experiments/, as a folder tree in 8 topics. A topic opens to its study folders, newest first. A study folder opens to its result pages, each with its size, the script in scripts/ that rebuilds it and its copy on claude.ai where they exist, and to a folder of the other files beside them: data, films, images, print files and bench plans. That folder lists two levels of subfolders and at most 12 files per folder besides the print files, with a link to the folder for the rest. The result pages, data and media are Git LFS files; the commands that fetch them are at the end.

    Fetching the pages and their files

    The result pages and the data and media files are Git LFS files, which open as a short pointer text until fetched; this index, the home page and the topic overviews are ordinary files and open from any clone. Every result page (268 MB):

    git lfs install
git -c lfs.fetchexclude= lfs pull --include="docs/experiments/**/*.html"

    One folder with its data and media, FOLDER being its dated name:

    git -c lfs.fetchexclude= lfs pull --include="docs/experiments/FOLDER/**"

    Each topic in the tree opens to the command that fetches its pages.

    Built by python3 scripts/docs_home_page.py from scripts/docs_inventory.py, which reads each result page's docs-* tags (topic, date, summary) and lede and the rows of docs/experiments/INDEX.md, and from the files git tracks under docs/experiments/; scripts/docs_index.py rebuilds it whenever a builder writes a result page.
